  These are PROPOSALS. Nothing here edits SERIES_TYPES, in either copy — that table
  is shared platform semantics read by every mmsell mode= book, so a change to it is
  a Platform Change Review event with its own impact review, not a side effect of an
  analysis script. A prefix marked INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE must stay unclassified: an
  in-play series wrongly recorded as scheduled would put in-play markets into the
  treatment arm and make the primary comparison measure the confound it controls for.
